Episodes are single rallies rather than full scored games, and training uses Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) self-play against a staged checkpoint opponent pool with sparse terminal rally-outcome rewards and a factor-specific recovery update. Evaluation with frozen checkpoint play, controlled tactical probes, recovery ablations, qualitative rollouts, and a human-data sanity check shows competitive improvement together with interpretable opponent-conditioned changes in shot geometry and recovery behavior. The learned policies produce recognizable badminton-like structure while also reflecting the abstractions of the simulator, and the recovery intervention shows that learned recovery behavior is competitively important. These results suggest that physics-based racket sports are a useful testbed for interactive digital entertainment AI because they require agents to coordinate execution, positioning, and opponent-relative tactical value.

</details>


### [274] [Neural-Bayesian Structure Learning for Discrete Choice Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25258)
*Hyunsoo Yun,Eun Hak Lee,Jiaru Zhang,Ziran Wang,Eui-Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Neural-BSL 框架，将可微结构学习与基于随机效用的离散选择模型耦合于同一可微流程中：将观测选择作为图外的备选方案效用比较，联合学习属性依赖结构与随机效用参数，并通过结构加权交互与拓扑序传播实现干预分析。在首尔SP数据和伦敦RP数据上，预测性能与常规基准相当，同时恢复出行为上一致的依赖结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散选择模型和机器学习模型主要基于观测数据估计，并将解释性协变量视为并行输入，缺乏内部机制来判定当某个属性被人为改变时，相关属性应如何联动调整；此外，互斥的选择结果（选择）可能扭曲所恢复的属性结构。因此需要一个能同时刻画属性间依赖并支持干预传播的结构学习框架。

Method: Neural-BSL 将可微结构学习与基于随机效用的离散选择估计耦合在单一可微流程中。为避免互斥选择结果扭曲结构恢复，观测选择被置于图之外，作为备选方案间的效用比较项；属性结构与随机效用参数被联合学习。学习得到的结构通过结构加权的属性交互进入选择模型，并为干预在下游属性中的传播提供拓扑序基础。干预评估流程为：更新被干预属性→按拓扑序传播模型隐含的下游变化→重算效用与选择概率，从而同时得到预测的方式分担响应及下游出行者/出行属性的变化。

Result: 在首尔陈述偏好（SP）数据与伦敦揭示偏好（RP）数据上，Neural-BSL 的预测性能与常规基准相当，同时能恢复行为上一致的依赖结构。在多种政策情景下，通过学习结构传播干预会改变各出行方式间的预测再分配，并揭示支撑这些响应的下游出行者与出行属性调整。

Conclusion: Neural-BSL 为离散选择建模提供了兼具预测能力与结构解释性的框架：它既能恢复行为上合理的属性依赖结构，又能支持结构化的干预分析，从而在政策评估中同时暴露方式再分配与下游属性调整，弥补了传统模型将属性视为并行输入、缺乏内部结构调整机制的不足。

Abstract: Conventional discrete choice and machine learning models are estimated primarily from observational data and typically treat explanatory covariates as parallel inputs, providing no internal mechanism for determining how related attributes should adjust when one is deliberately changed. This paper proposes Neural-Bayesian Structure Learning (Neural-BSL), a framework coupling differentiable structure learning with random-utility-based discrete choice estimation in a single differentiable procedure. To prevent mutually exclusive choice outcome from distorting the recovered attribute structure, the observed choice is maintained outside the graph as an alternative-specific utility comparison, while the attribute structure and random-utility parameters are learned jointly. The learned structure enters the choice model through structure-weighted attribute interactions and provides the structural basis for propagating interventions through downstream attributes. An intervention is evaluated by updating the intervened attribute, propagating its model-implied downstream changes in topological order, and then recomputing utilities and choice probabilities. This yields both predicted mode-share responses and the associated changes in downstream traveler or trip attributes. We evaluate Neural-BSL using stated-preference data from Seoul and the revealed-preference data from London. Neural-BSL achieves predictive performance comparable to conventional benchmarks while recovering behaviorally coherent dependency structures. Across policy scenarios, propagating interventions through the learned structure changes the predicted redistribution across modes while exposing the downstream traveler and trip adjustments underlying those responses.

</details>


### [275] [Mitigating LLM sycophancy with RL-based fine-tuning: Bayesian Truth Serum approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25267)
*Serhii Mytsyk,Yiming Zhang,Vikram Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需标签或偏好标注的谄媚（sycophancy）缓解方法：将贝叶斯真话血清（BTS）作为群组相对策略优化（GRPO）的奖励，对模型针对同一问题的多个回答组成的“群组”进行奖励，通过为“令人惊讶地普遍”的诚实回答支付奖励来抑制谄媚行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常表现出谄媚行为，即顺着用户陈述的信念或偏好作答而非报告自身认为为真的内容，这会降低事实准确性并放大错误信息；现有方法大多依赖标签或人工偏好标注，成本高且标注稀缺时难以应用。

Method: 将贝叶斯真话血清（BTS）这一同伴预测机制作为GRPO的奖励函数来微调LLM：BTS对“令人惊讶地普遍”的回答支付奖励，即回答在受访者中的实际频率高于受访者自身预测频率。作者把模型对同一问题的一组回答视为这些受访者，从而奖励仅取决于模型自身输出，无需标签。同时进行理论证明（大群体极限下谄媚回答的期望奖励严格低于诚实回答），并开展真/假基准实验、消融（与SMART、合成数据微调、pinpoint tuning比较）及机制分析（与Peer Truth Serum对比）。

Result: 在真/假基准上，参考模型在用户施压下的答案翻转率从23%降至4%，施压下的准确率从80%升至93%；所提奖励优于SMART，与使用标签训练的合成数据微调和pinpoint tuning效果相当，但计算开销显著更高，因此适用于标注稀缺场景。Peer Truth Serum（只为罕见答案支付溢价但不收集预测报告）可复现该效果，说明为更罕见答案支付的溢价是驱动效果的关键机制。

Conclusion: 在单个GRPO群组内计算的同伴预测奖励能在不依赖标签的情况下减少谄媚行为；比较不同机制表明，为更罕见答案支付的奖励溢价是产生该效果的主要驱动力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently exhibit \emph{sycophancy}: they adapt their answers to a user's stated beliefs or preferences instead of reporting what they hold to be true, which lowers factual accuracy and can amplify misinformation. This paper proposes a methodology for mitigating sycophancy that employs the Bayesian Truth Serum (BTS), a peer-prediction mechanism, as the reward in Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to fine-tune an LLM. BTS pays an answer for being \emph{surprisingly common}, that is, more frequent among respondents than those respondents themselves predicted. We treat a group of responses from a model for one question as those respondents, so the reward is a function of the model's own outputs and fine-tuning needs neither labels nor preference annotations. We prove that in the large-group limit a sycophantic response earns strictly lower expected reward than an honest one. We also prove that if the entire group agrees in advance on a symmetric answering rule, it cannot earn a higher information score than under truthful reporting. On our true/false benchmark the reference model's answer-flip rate under user pressure decreases from 23% to 4%, and its accuracy under that pressure increases from 80% to 93%. Our reward outperforms SMART and is comparable to synthetic-data fine-tuning and to pinpoint tuning, all three of which train on labels. It spends considerably more compute in exchange, which makes it suitable when labeled data is scarce. Peer Truth Serum, which also pays a premium for a rare answer but elicits no prediction report, reproduces the effect. A peer-prediction reward computed inside a single GRPO group therefore reduces sycophancy without labels, and comparing mechanisms suggests that the premium paid for a rarer answer drives the effect.

</details>


### [276] [SHSP: Structure-Aware Hierarchical Solution Prediction for Mixed-Integer Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25282)
*Zherong Zhang,Guanlin Li,Chengrui Gao,Haopu Shang,Ke Xue,Jixiang Lu,Weiyong Yang,Chao Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MILP求解加速中现有学习方法采用一次性并行预测、难以显式建模变量间条件依赖的问题，提出结构感知的分层解预测（SHSP）框架，通过按约束耦合强度递增的分层顺序解码并以上层预测为条件，结合置信度感知的掩码-修复机制抑制误差累积，在四个标准MILP基准上平均降低54%的解间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MILP具有NP-hard性质，大规模/强约束实例难以求解。现有基于学习的解预测方法多为一次性预测范式，并行地同时预测所有变量的边际概率，变量间的条件依赖仅通过消息传递隐式捕获，组合结构的建模负担完全落在图神经网络表示能力上，缺乏对变量间条件依赖的显式建模。

Method: 提出SHSP框架，用新颖的分层条件解码机制替代一次性方法的并行边际解码。具体地：1）从约束结构构建变量耦合图；2）按耦合强度递增的层级顺序逐层解码变量，每一层以先前预测的赋值结果为条件；3）引入置信度感知的掩码-修复机制，识别并纠正不可靠的中间预测，以缓解解码过程中的误差累积。SHSP可与多种学习引导搜索方法集成。

Result: 在四个标准MILP基准上与多种学习引导搜索方法集成评估，SHSP显著优于现有一次性预测基线，平均降低54%的解间隙。

Conclusion: 通过显式编码变量间的组合依赖关系并进行条件化顺序解码，SHSP有效克服了一次性预测范式难以建模结构依赖的局限，显著提升了解的质量与求解效率，验证了分层条件解码在MILP解预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) is a fundamental optimization paradigm in combinatorial optimization and has been widely applied across real-world domains. Due to its NP-hard nature, obtaining optimal solutions for large-scale or highly constrained MILP instances remains computationally prohibitive. Learning-based solution prediction has therefore emerged as a promising approach to provide high-quality variable assignment for solver acceleration. However, existing methods typically adopt a one-shot prediction paradigm that predicts the marginal probabilities of all variables simultaneously. As a result, the conditional dependencies among variables are only implicitly captured through message passing, with the burden of modeling the combinatorial structure falling entirely on the representational capacity of graph neural networks. To address this limitation, we propose the Structure-Aware Hierarchical Solution Prediction (SHSP) framework that replaces the parallel marginal decoding of one-shot methods with a novel hierarchical conditional decoding mechanism. Specifically, SHSP constructs a variable coupling graph from the constraint structure, decodes variables sequentially along a hierarchy of increasing coupling strength, and conditions each hierarchy on previously predicted assignments. To mitigate error accumulation during the decoding process, SHSP further incorporates a confidence-aware mask-and-repair mechanism to identify and correct unreliable intermediate predictions. We integrate SHSP with multiple learning-guided search methods, and evaluate it on four standard MILP benchmarks. Experimental results demonstrate that SHSP significantly outperforms existing one-shot prediction baselines, achieving a 54% average reduction in solution gap.

</details>


### [277] [InsightSR: Refining Symbolic Regression Search Spaces via Parallel Semantic and Structural LLM Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25291)
*Yating Ling,Wenjing Cun,Zhitang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: InsightSR 是一个将大语言模型（LLM）作为引导层嵌入 PySR 遗传编程引擎的符号回归框架。它通过语义种子路径（提出量纲一致的函数骨架）和结构特征路径（推荐非线性特征变换）逐步改造搜索空间，并借助后生成反馈循环不断优化引导，将符号发现从开放式生成转变为迭代式自校正的精炼过程。在 Feynman 基准上实现 95% 的精确恢复率，在 LLM-SRBench LSR-Transform 任务上达到 80.18% 的准确率，显著优于现有遗传编程与神经符号方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统符号回归方法（如遗传编程）在面对物理上有意义的表达式的巨大组合搜索空间时常常陷入困境，难以高效地发现简洁的数学定律。现有方法要么直接在原始变量上构造深层表达式树，搜索空间过大且容易产生物理上无意义的结果。

Method: InsightSR 将 LLM 作为 PySR 遗传编程引擎外层的引导机制，而非直接用 LLM 生成表达式。它通过两条互补路径渐进地改造搜索空间：语义种子路径提出量纲一致的函数骨架，结构特征路径推荐非线性特征变换；随着迭代累积，输入空间被拓宽，符号搜索从在原始变量上构造深层表达式树转变为在丰富的语义信息特征集上组装浅层树。此外，后生成反馈循环会对候选解进行评估、按经验效用对特征分类，并据此优化下一轮引导，形成迭代自校正的发现流程。

Result: 在三个基准测试上，InsightSR 在 Feynman 基准上达到 95% 的精确恢复率，在 LLM-SRBench LSR-Transform 任务上达到 80.18% 的准确率，大幅超越最先进的遗传编程和神经符号方法，并在真实世界数据集上保持良好的分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过 LLM 引导的搜索空间变换与迭代反馈精炼，InsightSR 有效缓解了符号回归的组合搜索难题，实现了更高的表达式精确恢复率与更强的泛化性能，验证了将 LLM 作为搜索空间引导层而非直接生成器的设计范式的有效性。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) seeks to discover parsimonious mathematical laws from observational data, yet conventional approaches often struggle with the vast combinatorial search space of physically meaningful expressions. We present InsightSR, a framework that embeds Large Language Models (LLMs) as a guiding layer around the PySR genetic programming engine. Rather than relying on LLMs to generate expressions directly, InsightSR uses LLMs to progressively transform the search space itself through two complementary pathways: a Semantic Seed Pathway that proposes dimensionally consistent functional skeletons, and a Structural Feature Pathway that recommends nonlinear feature transformations. These transformations accumulate over iterations, broadening the input space and shifting the symbolic search from constructing deep expression trees over raw variables to assembling shallow trees over a rich, semantically informed feature set. A post-generation feedback loop evaluates candidates, categorizes features by their empirical utility, and refines the guidance for the next iteration, transforming the discovery process from open-ended generation into iterative, self-correcting refinement. Across three benchmarks, InsightSR achieves a 95% exact recovery rate on the Feynman benchmark and 80.18% accuracy on the LLM-SRBench LSR-Transform task, substantially outperforming state-of-the-art genetic programming and neural-symbolic methods while maintaining strong out-of-distribution generalization on real-world datasets.

</details>


### [278] [Prefix-Denoising Consistency: Test-Time Verification for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25311)
*Yuki Ichihara,Naoto Iwase,Mohammad Atif Quamar,Junpei Komiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PDC（Prefix-Denoising Consistency），一种面向扩散语言模型（DLM）的测试时自验证方法。它利用前缀条件再生下的稳定性信号——正确轨迹比错误轨迹更稳定、可复现——通过将初始生成样本在中间位置拆分并基于固定前缀重新生成剩余 token，在数学推理与常识推理基准上持续提升初始样本质量，并优于在相同计算约束下的独立多次生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）近年来在性能上已可与自回归（AR）模型竞争，并能在某些任务上超越它们。与 AR 模型不同，DLM 通过迭代去噪生成输出，不遵循从左到右的顺序。为了进一步提升 DLM 的性能，需要利用 DLM 特有的验证信号进行测试时自验证，而现有的验证方法主要面向 AR 模型，缺少针对 DLM 生成机制的验证策略。

Method: PDC 是一种测试时自验证方法，其核心在于利用 DLM 在前缀条件再生下的特有信号：正确轨迹比错误轨迹更稳定且可复现。具体地，给定一个初始生成的样本，PDC 在句子的某个中间位置进行切分，然后以固定的前缀为条件重新生成剩余 token。

Result: 在数学推理和常识推理基准上，PDC 一致地提升了初始生成样本的性能；在计算受限的比较下，它优于独立多次生成；并且对不同的掩码（unmasking）策略和参数设置具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，前缀条件再生是 DLM 特有的一种有效的测试时验证原语，可作为提升扩散语言模型推理性能的通用而稳健的手段。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have recently become increasingly competitive with autoregressive (AR) models, and even outperform them on certain tasks. Unlike AR models, DLMs produce output through iterative denoising without a left-to-right order. To further improve the performance of DLMs, we introduce PDC (\emph{Prefix-Denoising Consistency}), a test-time self-verification method for DLMs. PDC exploits a distinctive test-time signal in DLMs under prefix conditioned regeneration, correct trajectories are more stable and reproducible than incorrect ones. Concretely, given an initially generated sample, PDC splits the sentence at an intermediate position and regenerates the remaining tokens conditioned on the fixed prefix. Across mathematical reasoning and commonsense reasoning benchmarks, PDC consistently improves upon the initial sample, outperforms independent generations under a computational constrained comparison, and is robust to different unmasking strategies and parameter settings. These results highlight prefix-conditioned regeneration as an effective DLM-specific primitive for test-time verification.

</details>


### [279] [Activation-Space Order-Swap Geometry: A Site-Asymmetry Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25315)
*Anqi Peter Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种“无拟合位点不对称审计”：在二次可微读出下，将开放路径的序交换向量分解为“单干预加性基线”与“反对称二阶差分”，并用六个开源语言模型族及视觉模型验证：基线解释序交换向量84.3–97.7%的范数，纠偏残差仅在三到两个模型中显著高于通用交互零假设，因此主张在把序交换向量解读为交互或几何结构前必须先运行单干预基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序依赖的激活统计常被当作网络中“交互”存在的证据，但这种解读会被干预进入网络的位置（site-asymmetry）所混淆——即单点干预的非线性/自曲率效应也可能制造出看似交互的序交换信号。

Method: 引入“无拟合位点不对称审计”。对二次可微读出，开放路径序交换可分解为：单干预测得的规范加性响应，加上反对称化的二阶差分（该差分在二阶上不含一阶项与纯自曲率项）。在两个族中使用正/负注入分割估计无交互自曲率项；用无混淆提示分割及配置稳健性检验纠偏残差；用已知阳性替代物植入混合交互作为验证，匹配位点分离、随机架构作为对照；并将同一估计器迁移到非语言参考模型（ViT-B/16与ResNet-50）。

Result: 六个开源权重语言模型族中，单干预基线解释括弧范数的84.3–97.7%（平均93.7%）；在采用正/负注入分割的两个族中，无交互自曲率项比纠偏残差大1.8–5.2倍。纠偏残差在无混淆提示分割下3/6个族、经配置稳健性后2/6个族通过通用交互零假设。已知阳性替代物能恢复植入的混合交互，匹配位点分离会改变基线占比，随机架构复现一阶主导机制。迁移到非语言参考模型时，训练残差比例在11/12个对比（ViT-B/16为5/6，ResNet-50为6/6）中低于固定高斯方向零假设，属于可移植性检验而非合并证据。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是一个可复用的测量准则：在把序交换向量解读为交互或几何结构之前，先运行单干预基线；若基线已能解释该向量，则应转而构造二阶差分。所有论断限于不同位点上的激活空间干预，不声称表征几何整体上是阿贝尔（可交换）的。

Abstract: Order-dependent activation statistics are often interpreted as evidence of interaction, but that interpretation can be confounded by where interventions enter the network. We introduce a no-fit site-asymmetry audit. For a twice-differentiable readout, the open-path order-swap decomposes into a canonical additive response measured by single interventions and an antisymmetrized second difference free of first-order and pure self-curvature terms to second order. Across six open-weight language-model families, the single-intervention baseline explains 84.3-97.7 percent of the bracket norm (mean 93.7 percent), while the no-interaction self-curvature term is 1.8-5.2 times larger than the corrected residual in the two families with the plus/minus injection split. The corrected residual clears a generic-interaction null in three of six families under a confound-free prompt split and two of six after configuration robustness. A known-positive surrogate recovers planted mixed interaction, while a matched site-separation test changes the baseline share and a random architecture reproduces the first-order regime. The same estimator transfers to released non-language references: trained residual fractions fall below a fixed Gaussian-direction null in 11/12 contrasts (5/6 ViT-B/16, 6/6 ResNet-50), a portability check rather than pooled evidence. The contribution is a reusable measurement criterion: run the single-intervention baseline before reading an order-swap vector as interaction or geometric structure; if it explains the vector, form the second difference instead. All claims are scoped to activation-space interventions at distinct sites; we do not claim that representation geometry is globally Abelian.

</details>


### [280] [Two Dimensions Govern Agnostic Multiclass Transductive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25326)
*Pahan Dewasurendra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明：在任意（可能无界）标签空间下，多类不可知转导学习的最优超额误差为 $\widetilde\Theta(d_{DS}/n+\sqrt{d_N/n})$，即由 DS 维数和 Natarajan 维数共同刻画，与 PAC 学习遵循相同的“二维律”（至多相差对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二分类情形下，不可知转导学习与 PAC 学习具有相同的极小极大率；但该结论能否推广到多类学习一直悬而未决，尤其是在标签空间无界、一致收敛可能失效的情况下，这一问题尤为关键。

Method: 采用随机预留（random-reservation）原则：学习器有意忽略一部分可见标签，使真实测试点在大型未观察块内近似均匀；随后结合可实现压缩、标签空间约简以及有限总体划分上的菜单内不可知压缩，并引入新的无放回乘性权重引理以保留快速项 $d_{DS}/n$。下界方面，用 DS 伪立方构造可实现 $d_{DS}/n$ 障碍，用带重复点与公平标签的 Natarajan 立方构造不可知 $\sqrt{d_N/n}$ 障碍。

Result: 对任意多类假设类 $\mathcal H$，不可知转导超额误差为 $\widetilde\Theta(d_{DS}/n+\sqrt{d_N/n})$，两项均不可省略；从而不可知多类 PAC 与转导学习在任意标签空间下服从同一二维律（对数因子内）。

Conclusion: 该结果将二分类中“转导与 PAC 等价”的现象推广到多类设定，并用 DS 维数与 Natarajan 维数给出了完整的极小极大刻画，解决了此前关于无界标签空间下一致收敛失效情形的开放问题。

Abstract: In transductive classification, an adversary fixes a labeled population, one label is hidden uniformly, and the learner sees all remaining labels. For binary classes, agnostic transductive and PAC learning have the same minimax rate. Whether this extends to multiclass learning was open, especially for unbounded label spaces where uniform convergence can fail. We resolve the question up to logarithmic factors. For every multiclass class $\mathcal H$ with DS dimension $d_{DS}$ and Natarajan dimension $d_{\mathrm N}$, the optimal agnostic transductive excess error satisfies $\widetildeΘ\left(\frac{d_{DS}}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{d_{\mathrm N}}{n}}\right).$ The result holds for arbitrary label spaces. The two terms are both necessary. A DS pseudo-cube gives the realizable $d_{DS}/n$ obstruction, while a Natarajan cube with repeated points and fair labels gives the agnostic $\sqrt{d_{\mathrm N}/n}$ obstruction. The upper bound uses a random-reservation principle. The learner deliberately ignores a constant fraction of the visible labels, which makes the true test point uniform in a large unseen block. We combine realizable compression, a label-space reduction, and inside-menu agnostic compression across this finite-population split. A new without-replacement multiplicative-weights lemma preserves the fast $d_{DS}/n$ term. Consequently, agnostic multiclass PAC and transductive learning obey the same two-dimension law up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [281] [Neither Precision Nor Architecture Alone: Controlled Tests of Failure Remedies for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25327)
*Jinyuan Zhang,Peng He,He Hu,Yin Yuan,ShengShuo Jiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过预先注册的大规模对照实验（144项 + 独立的85项），系统地检验了解决PINN在刚硬/对流主导PDE上失效问题的两类补救措施：FP32到FP64精度切换与SSM骨干+子序列对齐，发现二者作用于互不相交的“机制-种子”切片、均不能相互替代，且响应具有种子特异性，因此必须联合评估并按种子报告结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN在刚硬或被对流主导的偏微分方程上频繁失败，近期文献给出了两种相互竞争的补救方案：一是从FP32切换至FP64以修复L-BFGS停止假象，二是用状态空间模型（SSM）骨干替换MLP并加入子序列对齐以抵消架构的简单性偏差。然而这两种方案缺乏在受控条件下的系统比较，其真实效果、适用边界及相互关系尚不明确，因而需要一个公平的、匹配种子的系统实验来裁决。

Method: 在匹配且配对种子的受控条件下开展预先注册的144次运行研究（覆盖对流、反应和波动问题），并附带独立的85次运行对流/波动研究；以相对l2误差低于0.05作为成功标准。实验交叉组合了精度（FP32/FP64）、骨干（MLP/SSM）、是否进行子序列对齐等条件，并在独立事件中收紧内部L-BFGS容差以考察停止准则的影响，同时按种子逐一分析结果。

Result: 两种补救措施作用于互不相交的机制与种子切片：在对流困难情形（β=50）下，对齐使FP32中2/5、FP64中3/5的种子成功，而未对齐的SSM在两种精度下均为0/5，普通MLP仅从0/5提升到1/5，恢复主要归因于对齐目标而非骨干；在反应问题上，SSM骨干单独即可在3/5-4/5种子上成功，因此每种补救各覆盖另一补救无法覆盖的机制。响应具有种子特异性：同一精度切换使个别种子向相反方向翻转，在波动问题上降低中位误差但无统计显著的成功率增益；收紧L-BFGS容差虽降低中位误差但代价是巨大运行时间，且成功计数不变。

Conclusion: 精度、停止准则、骨干架构与对齐机制必须被联合评估，并且结果应按种子逐一报告；单一补救措施（无论精度提升还是架构更换）都无法全面解决PINN在这些PDE上的失败问题，其有效性高度依赖具体机制与种子。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) frequently fail on stiff or advection-dominated PDEs, and two recent accounts offer competing remedies: switching from FP32 to FP64 to repair an L-BFGS stopping artifact, or replacing the MLP with a state-space-model (SSM) backbone plus sub-sequence alignment to counter architectural simplicity bias. We test both under matched, seed-paired controls in a pre-registered 144-run study spanning convection, reaction, and wave, plus an independent 85-run convection/wave study; success is relative $\ell_2$ error below $0.05$. The two remedies act on disjoint regime-and-seed slices: neither substitutes for the other. On hard convection ($β{=}50$), alignment recovers 2/5 seeds in FP32 and 3/5 in FP64, where the unaligned SSM succeeds on 0/5 seeds at either precision and the vanilla MLP moves only from 0/5 to 1/5 across the precision switch---the recoveries trace to the alignment objective, not the backbone. On reaction the backbone alone already succeeds on 3/5--4/5 seeds, so each remedy covers a regime the other does not. Responses are also seed-specific: the same precision switch flips individual seeds in opposite directions and, on wave, lowers median error with no statistically significant success gain. Tightening the inner L-BFGS tolerance in an independent repeated-step runner likewise lowers median error at a large runtime cost, with success counts unchanged. Precision, stopping, backbone, and alignment must therefore be evaluated jointly and reported per seed.

</details>


### [282] [Beyond Pairwise Feedback: Listwise Vision-Language Supervision for Preference-Based Reward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25350)
*Srivalli Katkuri,Maxwell Kawada,Juan Wachs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个将视觉语言模型（VLM）生成偏好与Plackett-Luce（PL）模型相结合的奖励学习框架，用于强化学习。该框架利用VLM对多个候选结果进行列表式排序，而非传统的两两对比（Bradley-Terry），从而更充分地发挥VLM的排序能力。在Meta-World操作任务上的实验表明，PL奖励模型与成对Bradley-Terry、K-wise Bradley-Terry及RL-VLM-F基线效果相当，且至少一种PL排序大小（K∈{3,4,5}）在所有环境中平均成功率均持平或优于其他方法；最佳PL配置达到86%的平均最终成功率，并在Drawer Open任务上匹配Oracle基线，证明了列表式VLM偏好监督是一种有竞争力且灵活的奖励学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）为强化学习提供了强大的监督信号。现有方法借鉴基于偏好的奖励学习（PbRL），通过VLM对两个结果进行视觉对比并采用Bradley-Terry（BT）模型学习奖励函数。然而，这种成对公式每次仅利用两个观测样本，而VLM实际上有能力对多个候选结果进行排序。因此，作者希望引入Plackett-Luce（PL）公式，以列表式排序替代成对偏好，使VLM的排序能力得到更充分的利用。

Method: 作者提出首个将VLM生成偏好与Plackett-Luce模型结合的奖励学习框架：利用VLM对多个候选轨迹/结果进行列表式排序，基于PL模型从这些列表式排序中学习奖励函数。与限于K=2的成对方法不同，PL支持不同的排序规模K∈{3,4,5}，可适应不同环境和所需反馈格式。实验在Meta-World操作任务上评估，并与Bradley-Terry、K-wise Bradley-Terry及RL-VLM-F基线进行对比。

Result: 实验表明，PL奖励模型在Meta-World任务上能以VLM生成排序训练机器人策略，效果与成对Bradley-Terry、K-wise Bradley-Terry及RL-VLM-F基线相当。在所有环境中，至少有一个PL排序规模（K∈{3,4,5}）的平均成功率持平或优于其他方法。最佳PL配置达到86%的平均最终成功率，并在Drawer Open任务上匹配Oracle基线。

Conclusion: 列表式VLM偏好监督是一种有竞争力且灵活的奖励学习方法。PL模型通过支持可变的排序规模K，克服了成对方法仅限K=2的局限，可根据环境和期望的反馈格式进行调整，为强化学习中的奖励学习提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have emerged as a powerful source of supervision for reinforcement learning, enabling agents to leverage rich semantic knowledge during training. Inspired by the success of preference-based reward learning (PbRL) in reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), vision-language model generated image-based preferences provide an effective source for learning reward functions. This can be done by visually comparing two outcomes through the Bradley-Terry (BT) model. However, this pairwise formulation utilizes only two observations at a time, despite VLMs being capable of ranking multiple candidates. The Plackett-Luce (PL) formulation can shape a reward model with listwise rankings as opposed to pairwise preferences, allowing for a more suited use of a VLM based ranking. In this work, to our knowledge, we introduce the first framework that combines VLM-generated preferences with the Plackett-Luce model for reward learning. We evaluate our approach on Meta-World manipulation tasks and show that Plackett-Luce (PL) reward models can train robotic policies from VLM-generated rankings as effectively as pairwise Bradley-Terry, $K$-wise Bradley-Terry, and RL-VLM-F baselines. Across all environments, at least one PL ranking size ($K \in \{3,4,5\}$) consistently performs with or outperforms other methods in mean success rate. Unlike pairwise methods, which are restricted to $K=2$, PL supports different ranking sizes and can therefore be adapted to the environment and desired feedback format. Our best PL configuration achieves an 86% mean final success rate and matches the Oracle baseline on Drawer Open. Overall, these results demonstrate that listwise VLM preference supervision is a competitive and flexible approach to reward learning for reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [283] [Escaping Low-Dimensional Overlap: Multi-Task Model Merging via High-Dimensional Sparse Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25354)
*Yihang Zhang,Shengke Sun,Junjie Wen,Feng Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于稀疏表示（Sparse Autoencoders, SAEs）的模型合并框架，将任务向量投影到高维稀疏特征空间实现特征级解耦，并引入轻量级分组秩零阶优化器（GR-ZOO）识别关键层进行选择性合并。在Qwen2.5-1.5B和Qwen2.5-7B上，该方法在数学推理、代码生成、指令跟随和通用知识等任务上一致超越Task Arithmetic、TIES-Merge、DARE、Fisher-Merge及多种近期免训练合并方法，高冲突四任务场景下相对最强基线提升2.78%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并能将多个任务专家模型高效合成通用多任务模型而无需额外训练，但严重的任务干扰会导致性能退化。任务干扰主要源于"叠加"（superposition）现象——任务特定特征在参数空间中相互纠缠，使得传统分解方法难以有效分离有用任务方向与干扰成分。

Method: 提出基于稀疏表示的合并框架：1) 使用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）将任务向量投影到高维稀疏特征空间，在融合前实现特征级解耦；2) 为降低计算开销，引入轻量级分组秩零阶优化器（GR-ZOO）来识别任务关键层，实现选择性合并。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-1.5B和Qwen2.5-7B上的实验表明，该方法在数学推理、代码生成、指令跟随和通用知识任务上一致超越Task Arithmetic、TIES-Merge、DARE、Fisher-Merge及多种近期免训练合并方法；在Qwen2.5-1.5B的高冲突四任务场景下，相对最强基线取得2.78%的提升。

Conclusion: SAE稀疏特征空间的表示学习方法能有效缓解模型合并中的叠加/任务干扰问题，结合GR-ZOO层选择可兼顾性能与计算效率，为多任务通用模型的免训练合并提供了一种更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Model merging provides an efficient way to construct multi-task generalist models without additional training, but its performance often degrades under severe task interference. Task interference in model merging primarily stems from \textit{superposition}, where task-specific features become entangled within the parameter space. This entanglement renders conventional decomposition methods insufficient for effectively isolating useful task directions from interfering components. In this paper, we propose a sparse-representation-based merging framework that uses Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to project task vectors into a high-dimensional sparse feature space, enabling feature-level disentanglement before fusion. To reduce computational overhead, we further introduce a lightweight Group-Ranked Zeroth-Order Optimizer (GR-ZOO) to identify task-critical layers for selective merging. Experiments on both Qwen2.5-1.5B and Qwen2.5-7B demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms representative baselines, including Task Arithmetic, TIES-Merge, DARE, Fisher-Merge,and several recent training-free merging methods, across mathematical reasoning, code generation, instruction following, and general knowledge tasks. In a highly conflicting four-task setting on Qwen2.5-1.5B, our method further achieves a 2.78\% improvement over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [284] [PaSta: Noisy Node Classification with Partial Label Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25365)
*Yujing Liu,Yixin Liu,Yu Zheng,Yue Tan,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于部分标签的自训练框架 PaSta，用于解决图上的噪声节点分类问题。该方法通过多个标注器聚合生成高质量部分标签，设计双损失函数同时在标签空间和表示空间引导模型学习，并引入闭环迭代的自训练策略优化标注器，在五个数据集上平均提升 1.1% 的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噪声节点分类是图相关网络服务中基础且具有挑战性的任务，节点标签常因弱监督或自动标注而损坏不可靠。现有方法基于 one-hot 标签训练模型，不仅容易对噪声标签过拟合，还会在伪标签引导的增强过程中造成错误累积。

Method: 提出 PaSta 框架：1) 训练多个标注器以综合捕获节点类别分布，并聚合其预测构建高质量部分标签；2) 设计基于部分标签的分类模型，包含两个精心设计的损失函数，分别在标签空间和表示空间引导模型学习；3) 引入自训练策略，将经部分标签学习精炼后的标签用于闭环迭代地进一步优化标注器，增强对噪声标签的鲁棒性。

Result: 在五个数据集上的大量实验表明，与现有最先进方法相比，PaSta 在各种噪声设置下平均提升 1.1% 的分类性能。

Conclusion: PaSta 借助部分标签学习技术有效克服了基于 one-hot 标签训练方法的局限性，通过多标注器聚合、双空间损失约束与闭环自训练，显著提升了对噪声标签的鲁棒性和节点分类性能。

Abstract: Noisy node classification problem is a fundamental yet challenging task for real-world graph-related web services, where node labels are often corrupted or unreliable due to weak supervision or automatic annotation. However, existing methods typically train models based on one-hot labels, which not only makes models susceptible to overfitting on noisy labels, but also leads to error accumulation after pseudo-label-guided enhancement. In this paper, we propose a novel Partial label-based Self-training framework (PaSta for short) that leverages partial label learning technique to overcome the limitations of existing methods. Specifically, PaSta first trains multiple annotators to comprehensively capture the class distribution of nodes and aggregates their predictions to construct high-quality partial labels. Subsequently, we design a partial label-based classification model with two well-crafted loss functions to guide the model learning at both label and representation spaces. To further enhance the robustness against noisy labels, we introduce a self-training strategy where the labels refined by partial label learning are then used to further optimize the annotators in a closed-loop iterative manner. Extensive experiments on five datasets demonstrate that, compared with existing state-of-the-art methods, PaSta achieves an average improvement of 1.1% in classification performance under various noise settings.

</details>


### [285] [Refusal geometry reflects refusal training: diverse refusal prefixes can raise stable rank and weaken refusal vector ablation attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25390)
*Andrey Labunets*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以OLMo-2-0425-1B-Instruct为案例，研究拒绝训练为何会形成低维的拒绝子空间，发现拒绝方向与拒绝子空间可由拒绝补全首个token的激活更新（梯度）解释，并揭示拒绝行为的脆弱性与重复的拒绝开头词相关；相反，多样化的拒绝开头可提高梯度和激活变化的稳定秩，增强模型对向量消融攻击的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作发现对齐语言模型中的拒绝行为可由单个激活方向或低维拒绝子空间介导，但对为何安全关键特征会在广泛模型中涌现并集中于低维结构仍不清楚。

Method: 以OLMo-2-0425-1B-Instruct为案例，结合训练动态分析、冻结模型分析以及受控的合成微调实验，研究拒绝方向的形成机制；通过对比不同拒绝数据集中的梯度与激活更新，检验拒绝开头的多样性如何影响梯度与拒绝特征的秩。

Result: 发现拒绝方向与拒绝子空间的几何结构源于拒绝补全首个token损失产生的激活更新；重复的拒绝开头导致梯度与拒绝特征在低维子空间集中，从而使拒绝行为脆弱；而多样化的拒绝开头能够提升梯度和激活变化的稳定秩，使拒绝更难通过向量消融攻击被移除（"硬化杠杆"）。

Conclusion: 拒绝行为的低维集中结构是拒绝训练本身的直接产物，其脆弱性源于训练数据中拒绝开头的重复性；通过引入多样化的拒绝开头可提升拒绝特征的空间秩，从而增强模型对消融类越狱攻击的鲁棒性，为安全对齐训练提供了可操作的改进方向。

Abstract: Refusal training protects AI models from jailbreaks by training models to decline unsafe queries, reducing the risk of misuse. Recent work finds that refusal behavior in aligned language models can be mediated by a single activation direction or a low-dimensional refusal subspace shared across harmful prompts: ablating those directions suppresses refusals while largely preserves other model capabilities. Yet it remains unclear why safety-critical features in a wide range of models emerge and concentrated, low-dimensional structure. In a case study of OLMo-2-0425-1B-Instruct we find that the refusal geometry reflects refusal training: activation updates resulting from refusal-completion first-token losses explain the resulting refusal direction and refusal subspace. We study refusal directions through the training dynamics across refusal datasets and reveal that their brittleness is associated with repetitive refusal starts, which in turn is linked to concentration of gradients and refusal features in a low-dimensional subspace. Across frozen-model analyses and controlled synthetic fine-tuning, we find evidence of a hardening lever: diverse refusal starts can raise stable ranks of gradients and activation changes, making refusals harder to remove with a vector ablation attack.

</details>


### [286] [Joint Initialization of Flux Networks and Effective Multiplication Factor for Physics-Informed Neural Networks Solving Neutron Diffusion Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25443)
*Qin Hang,Yangdi Yi,Jiayi Li,Xu Wang,Heng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出联合初始化物理信息神经网络（JI-PINN），利用K特征值问题的低分辨率近似解构建通量网络参数与keff的联合初始状态，并在物理约束下联合优化，从而更高效、更稳健地求解中子扩散K特征值问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在反应堆堆芯中子学分析中，高效求解有效增殖因子（keff）至关重要。虽然PINN已能结合中子扩散方程与边界条件求解中子通量分布和keff，但计算效率仍有提升空间，因此需要一种更高效的初始化策略来加速keff计算。

Method: 提出JI-PINN方法：先获取K特征值问题的低分辨率近似解，据此为通量网络参数与keff构造联合初始状态，随后在物理约束（中子扩散方程和边界条件）下对二者进行联合优化。

Result: 在二维双群双材料、IAEA 2D基准、二维双群四材料、三维单群等算例上，总计算时间分别降低25.4%、38.2%、49.4%和28.9%，同时保持相当的求解精度，并减少了keff显著偏离参考值的异常结果出现。

Conclusion: JI-PINN为使用PINN求解中子扩散K特征值问题提供了一种更高效、更稳健的初始化策略，在提高计算效率的同时改善了结果的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Efficient determination of the effective multiplication factor (keff) is an important computational task in reactor core neutronics analysis. Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) incorporate neutron diffusion equations and boundary conditions into network training to efficiently determine the neutron flux distribution and keff. To further improve the efficiency of keff calculations using PINNs, a Joint Initialization Physics-Informed Neural Network (JI-PINN) is proposed in this work. In this method, a low-resolution approximate solution to the K-eigenvalue problem is used to construct a joint initial state for the flux network parameters and keff, and both are then jointly optimized under physical constraints. The proposed method was validated on a two-dimensional two-group two-material case, the IAEA 2D benchmark, a two-dimensional two-group four-material case, and a three-dimensional single-group case. For these test cases, the total computational time was reduced by 25.4%, 38.2%, 49.4%, and 28.9%, respectively, while comparable solution accuracy was maintained. The occurrence of anomalous results associated with marked deviations of keff from the reference value was also reduced. The proposed method provides a more efficient and robust initialization strategy for solving neutron diffusion K-eigenvalue problem with PINNs.

</details>


### [287] [Resolving Multi-Modal Regression by Difference-Quotient-Based Clustering:Fast Coarse Conditional-Label Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25467)
*Huang Weiquan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态回归中的均值坍缩问题，提出差分商聚类（DQC）方法：通过最小化簇内输出-输入差异对数据进行硬聚类，为每个样本分配使最大矛盾比最小的簇标签，再训练 logits 生成器与条件网络；测试时以最小均方误差（minMSE）评估。实验表明 DQC 显著优于均值坍缩与随机标签，逼近理论 oracle。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平方损失下无约束回归器会收敛到条件均值，当模态数 K>1 时该均值远离所有模态，导致多模态回归的均值坍缩病态；其根源是成对矛盾——输入几乎相同但输出相距甚远的样本对。

Method: 提出差分商聚类（DQC）：划分数据以最小化簇内输出-输入差异，每个样本被分配到使其最大矛盾比最小的簇；随后在所得标签上训练 logits 生成器与条件网络。聚类作为硬、可并行化、复杂度 O(n^2/2) 的前端，为下游生成式细化提供粗粒度条件分配。

Result: 在合成基准（K=5,10）上，DQC 的测试 minMSE 为 0.19（K=5，nx=500），优于随机标签的 1.08 和均值坍缩的 1.33，接近 oracle 的 0.09。另发现两条经验规律：簇内矛盾越大需越深的网络；oracle 标签比簇派生标签需要更少样本即可泛化。

Conclusion: DQC 以聚类方式有效缓解均值坍缩，显著优于基线并接近 oracle，验证了"先粗聚类后细生成"的可行性；作者将基于残差误差的二阶段重聚类列为未来工作。

Abstract: Multimodal regression suffers from the mean-collapse pathology: under squared loss, an unconstrained regressor converges to the conditional mean, which for K > 1 lies away from all modes. We attribute this failure to pairwise contradictions--samples with nearly identical inputs but distant outputs--and propose Difference-Quotient Clustering (DQC), which partitions data to minimize intra-cluster output-vs-input discrepancy. Each sample is assigned to the cluster that minimizes its maximum contradiction ratio; a logits generator and a conditional network are then trained on the resulting labels. Since the generating modality is unknown at test time, we evaluate via minimum squared error (minMSE) against all K true outputs. On synthetic benchmarks (K=5, 10), DQC achieves test minMSE 0.19 (K=5, nx=500), versus 0.09 for an oracle, 1.08 for random labels, and 1.33 for mean collapse. We observe two empirical regularities: larger intra-cluster contradictions require deeper networks, and oracle labels generalize from fewer samples than cluster-derived equivalents. The clustering is a hard, parallelizable O(n^2/2) front-end for coarse conditional assignment, reducing the burden of downstream generative refinement. A second-stage re-clustering on residual errors is outlined as future work.

</details>


### [288] [A Storage-Retrieval Gap in Parametric Knowledge Graph Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25489)
*Martino M. L. Pulici,Cuong Xuan Chu,Evgeny Kharlamov,Volker Tresp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将知识图谱离线编译为按实体组织的 LoRA 适配器库（参数化知识层），在查询时通过注入权重而非文本进行检索，实现零查询时上下文开销。实验表明，基于子图训练的适配器能编码可泛化的上下文无关事实知识（在单值关系上相对于近乎失明的基座模型提升 +0.243 精确匹配，仅正确的适配器可恢复该知识），但基于相似度的检索（嵌入或权重空间几何）均无法恢复知识（等同随机），因为知识局部存储且不跨实体迁移；权重几何虽与子图语义相关（ρ=+0.329），却与功能可检索性无关。作者量化了与图RAG的字节/上下文令牌成本，并指出"如何以语义相似度之外的方式选择和组合适配器"是核心开放问题，从而引出学习式的、以查询为条件的组合机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图检索增强生成（Graph RAG）在每次查询时都将检索到的子图放入模型的上下文窗口，导致重复的令牌开销并在每次调用时暴露源数据。作者希望探索另一种方案：将知识图谱离线编译为参数化记忆（LoRA适配器库），使知识存储于权重而非上下文中，从而消除查询时的上下文成本并缓解数据暴露问题。

Method: 将知识图谱按实体切分为子图，为每个实体训练一个 LoRA 适配器，构成"每实体一适配器"的参数化知识层；查询时注入对应适配器的权重而非文本。在 MetaQA 数据集上，对比适配器方法（含 oracle 选择、基于嵌入相似度的检索、基于权重空间几何的检索）与图 RAG，评估精确匹配得分、可检索性与成本（字节数与上下文令牌数）。

Result: 子图训练的适配器能够编码上下文无关事实知识并可泛化到未见问题：单值关系上适配器相对基座模型（封闭书形式几乎失明，得 0.007）获得 +0.243 的精确匹配提升，只有正确的适配器能恢复该知识（oracle 相对基座提升 +0.283）。然而，基于嵌入或权重空间几何的相似度检索均表现如随机水平——语义邻近实体的适配器并不包含答案，知识为局部存储且不迁移。权重几何与子图语义相关（ρ=+0.329），但与功能可检索性无关。作者还量化了相对图 RAG 的字节与上下文令牌成本。

Conclusion: 参数化知识图谱记忆在存储知识方面是可行的，但核心开放问题在于：需要一种不同于语义相似度的机制来选择和组合正确的适配器，这为学习式的、以查询为条件的适配器组合机制指明了方向；同时该方法在成本与隐私方面的优势使其具有实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Graph retrieval-augmented generation places retrieved subgraphs into the model's context window at query time, paying a recurring token cost and exposing source data on every call. We study an alternative: compiling a knowledge graph offline into a bank of LoRA adapters, one per entity, that serve as a parametric knowledge layer queried by injecting weights rather than text, at zero query-time context cost. On the MetaQA dataset, we find that subgraph-trained adapters encode context-free factual knowledge that generalizes to unseen questions: on single-valued relations the adapter gains $+0.243$ exact-match score over a base model that is nearly blind closed-book ($0.007$), and only the correct adapter recovers this knowledge (an oracle gap of $+0.283$ over the base model). However, the stored knowledge is not recoverable by similarity: given a query with no subgraph, embedding-based and weight-space geometry retrieval both perform at chance, because a semantically neighbouring entity's adapter does not contain the answer - knowledge is stored locally and does not transfer. Weight geometry correlates with subgraph semantics ($ρ= +0.329$) but not with functional retrievability. We quantify the byte and context-token costs against graph retrieval-augmented generation and discuss deployment implications. Our results establish that parametric knowledge graph memory is feasible for storing knowledge, and identify selecting and composing the right adapters by a mechanism other than semantic similarity as the central open problem - motivating a learned, query-conditioned composition mechanism.

</details>


### [289] [FedQoS: Federated QoS-Risk Learning for Heterogeneous Indoor-Outdoor Access Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25496)
*Nguyen Van Thieu,Ti Ti Nguyen,Ons Aouedi,Zerihun Huruy,Vu Nguyen Ha,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FedQoS，一种面向动态异构室内外无线环境的联邦 QoS 风险学习框架，通过本地学习与联邦聚合预测候选接入链路的 QoS 失效概率，在不集中用户级数据的前提下实现可靠的接入节点选择，显著降低 QoS 失效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态且异构的室内外环境中，仅依赖瞬时无线测量无法捕捉由移动性、遮挡、流量负载和资源竞争导致的未来 QoS 恶化；同时集中式收集用户级网络数据又存在隐私问题，因此需要一种既能预测未来链路可靠性、又能保持数据本地化的接入选择机制。

Method: FedQoS 框架中，每个接入节点基于其观测到的网络日志（包含无线、流量、负载和服务上下文特征）进行本地学习，通过联邦聚合训练全局 QoS 风险预测器。该模型估计每个候选链路的 QoS 失效概率，控制器据此风险评分在动态网络条件下选择可靠接入节点。评估使用基于 Sionna 框架构建的物理仿真室内外无线数据集，涵盖正常流量、移动性、事件驱动拥塞及非 IID 客户端观测。

Result: 仿真结果表明，基于学习的接入选择相比基于信号强度和基于历史 QoS 的启发式方法显著降低了 QoS 失效率；FedQoS 达到接近集中式训练的预测性能，在轻度非 IID 数据下提供明显的可靠性增益，在更具挑战性的严重非 IID 条件下仍保持有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了联邦 QoS 风险学习在动态无线环境中实现可靠、数据本地化接入选择的潜力，为兼顾隐私与 QoS 保障的接入管理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Reliable access selection in dynamic and heterogeneous indoor-outdoor environments is challenging because instantaneous radio measurements alone cannot capture future QoS degradation caused by mobility, blockage, traffic load, and resource competition. This paper proposes FedQoS, a federated QoS-risk learning framework for predicting the future reliability of candidate access links and supporting access-node selection without centralizing user-level network data. In FedQoS, each access node locally learns from its observed network logs, including radio, traffic, load, and service-context features, while a global QoS-risk predictor is trained through federated aggregation. The learned model estimates the probability of QoS failure for each candidate link, and the controller uses these risk scores to select reliable access nodes under dynamic network conditions. To evaluate the framework, we construct physics-based synthetic indoor-outdoor wireless datasets using the Sionna framework, covering normal traffic, mobility, event-driven congestion, and non-IID client observations. Simulation results show that learning-based access selection substantially reduces the QoS-failure rate compared with signal-based and historical-QoS heuristic methods. FedQoS achieves near-centralized predictive performance and provides clear reliability gains under mild non-IID data while remaining competitive under the more challenging severe non-IID condition. These results demonstrate the potential of federated QoS-risk learning for reliable, data-local access selection in dynamic wireless environments.

</details>


### [290] [Resilient Decentralized Wireless Federated Learning via Gradient Tracking with AdamW](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25535)
*Nguyen Van Thieu,Ti Ti Nguyen,Ons Aouedi,Vu Nguyen Ha,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QEF-GT-AdamW，一种面向无线物联网边缘网络的通信高效且抗中断的分布式学习算法。它结合梯度跟踪、AdamW自适应优化以及带误差反馈的双流偏置量化，并采用本地回退策略处理不可靠广播，在异构数据和受限无线通信下建立了收敛保证，并在异构MNIST和CIFAR-10上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线IoT边缘网络需要能够在异构本地数据和通信受限链路下可靠运行的分布式学习方法，但现有分布式优化方案在严格的空中时间预算、衰落信道和数据包丢失条件下会产生大量通信开销并导致性能下降。

Method: 提出QEF-GT-AdamW算法，将梯度跟踪（缓解非IID数据影响）、AdamW自适应优化（提升训练稳定性）与带误差反馈的双流偏置量化（降低模型与跟踪信息交换的通信负载）相结合，并引入本地回退策略应对不可靠的广播通信；同时显式建模带宽、发射功率、空中时间约束与衰落信道对分布式学习性能的影响。

Result: 在异构MNIST和CIFAR-10设置下，QEF-GT-AdamW较代表性DecL基线在鲁棒性和收敛性能上持续提升，并在受限无线资源下取得有利的精度-通信权衡；同时针对压缩和不可靠无线通信建立了收敛保证。

Conclusion: QEF-GT-AdamW是一种通信高效、抗中断的分布式学习算法，能够同时在异构数据和不可靠无线通信条件下保持良好收敛与传输效率，为无线IoT边缘网络提供了一种可靠且实用的DecL解决方案。

Abstract: Wireless Internet-of-Things (IoT) edge networks require decentralized learning (DecL) methods that can operate reliably under both heterogeneous local data and communication-constrained wireless links. However, existing decentralized optimization schemes often incur substantial communication overhead and degraded performance when transmissions are constrained by strict airtime budgets, fading channels, and packet losses. This paper proposes QEF-GT-AdamW, a communication-efficient and outage-resilient algorithm for DecL over wireless communication (WCom) networks. The proposed method combines gradient tracking to mitigate the effect of non-IID data, AdamW-based adaptive optimization to improve training stability, and dual-stream biased quantization with error feedback to reduce communication payloads for both model and tracking exchanges. To address unreliable broadcast communication, the proposed framework further employs a local fallback strategy when scheduled packets are not successfully received. We explicitly model the effect of bandwidth, transmit power, airtime constraints, and fading channels on DecL performance, and establish convergence guarantees for the proposed algorithm under compressed and unreliable wireless communication. Experimental results on heterogeneous MNIST and CIFAR-10 settings show that QEF-GT-AdamW consistently improves robustness and convergence performance over representative DecL baselines while achieving favorable accuracy-communication trade-offs under limited wireless resources.

</details>


### [291] [Reflection Steering: Disentangling Reflection from Reasoning in Activation Space for Token-Efficient Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25542)
*Jiarui Hu,Zhiyuan Wen,Xiaoyun Liu,Jiaxing Shen,Yu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Reflection Steering，一种无需训练的激活引导框架，通过将反思相关激活与通用推理激活解耦，控制大语言模型中的反思计算量，在保持准确率的同时平均减少16.9%的推理token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型常生成包含验证、修订和回溯的推理轨迹，而单纯的“重新检查既有结果”式反思会浪费推理token并增加延迟。现有反思引导方法在预设层上添加由标签推导的均值差方向，但该方向与推理信号和长度信号纠缠，导致准确率-效率权衡不稳定，因此需要一种能解耦反思信号与通用推理信号的引导机制。

Method: 在LLM的每一层对比反思与非反思隐藏状态，用PCA对得到的反思方向去噪，并将其与通用推理方向正交化以解耦两类信号；为限制早期层干预引发的下游放大效应，在一小组数据上用多种干预强度校准每层，仅保留稳定的层，并对残差流激活施加有界的投影移除；此外引入有界的反思干预强度参数α，支持部署时动态调节。

Result: 在两个公开基准和三个开源权重LLM上的大量实验表明，该方法在六个匹配设置下平均减少16.9%的推理token，优于最先进的激活引导基线；α参数可在部署时平衡token节省、准确率与生成稳定性。

Conclusion: Reflection Steering能够以解耦、稳定且可调的免训练方式控制LLM中的反思相关计算，实现了token节省、准确率与生成稳定性之间的灵活权衡。

Abstract: Large reasoning models often produce reasoning traces with verification, revision, and backtracking. When reflection merely re-checks established results, it wastes reasoning tokens and increases latency. Most existing reflection steering methods add a label-derived mean-difference direction across preset layers, but its entanglement with reasoning and length signals destabilizes the accuracy-efficiency trade-off. In this paper, we propose Reflection Steering, a training-free framework for controlling reflection-associated computation within LLMs by disentangling reflection-related activations from general reasoning. Specifically, we contrast reflective and non-reflective hidden states at each LLM layer, denoise the resulting reflection directions with PCA, and orthogonalize them against general-reasoning directions. To limit downstream amplification from early-layer interventions, we calibrate each layer across multiple intervention strengths on a small set, retain only stable layers, and apply bounded projection removal to their residual-stream activations. We conduct extensive experiments across two public benchmarks and three open-weight LLMs against state-of-the-art activation-steering baselines. Results show that Reflection Steering reduces reasoning tokens by 16.9% on average across six matched settings. Besides, our method further introduces a bounded reflection intervention-strength parameter $α$, enabling deployment-time adjustment to balance token savings, accuracy, and generation stability.

</details>


### [292] [Interpreting Protein Language Model Embeddings via Orthogonal Projection for Protein Fitness Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25548)
*Paulo Yanez Sarmiento,Pia Francesca Rissom,Manuel Pfeuffer,Marco Simnacher,Jordan F. Safer,Sumaiya Iqbal,Henrike O. Heyne,Nadja Klein,Bernhard Y. Renard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于正交投影的可解释性分析方法，通过从蛋白质语言模型（PLM）嵌入中移除已知生化表格特征（包括高阶与交互效应），量化这些可解释生化特征对蛋白质适应性预测的贡献，并通过消融实验与方差分析证明PLM嵌入编码了与生化性质相关的模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质语言模型（PLM）嵌入在蛋白质适应性预测等下游任务上表现优异，但其嵌入不可直接解释，人们不清楚嵌入中编码了哪些特征。为理解是哪些生化性质驱动了预测，需要一种方法来剖析并量化嵌入与可解释生化特征之间的关系。

Method: 利用正交投影技术去除嵌入中已知表格化生化特征的线性效应，并将其扩展至高阶效应与交互效应，从而从PLM嵌入中剥离可解释的生化特征；随后通过消融实验（仅用处理后的嵌入训练下游分类器）和方差分解评估这些特征对蛋白质适应性预测的贡献。

Result: 消融实验表明，去除这些生化特征效应后，下游蛋白质适应性分类器的性能下降；进一步评估发现生化特征解释了分类器预测中的相当一部分方差。因此证明了PLM嵌入编码了与生化性质相关的模式，并量化了其对适应性预测的贡献。

Conclusion: 该正交投影方法计算高效、不限于特定特征或嵌入，可推广到蛋白质适应性预测之外的多种问题场景，为PLM嵌入的可解释性提供了通用分析手段。

Abstract: Recently, there has been a growing adoption of protein language models (PLMs) in biomedical science. Their embeddings provide a rich numerical representation of protein sequences which achieve state-of-the-art performance on several downstream tasks including protein fitness prediction. However, PLM embeddings are not directly interpretable and, thereby, it remains unclear what features they encode. To gain insight into which biochemical properties of the protein are driving the prediction, we leverage an orthogonal projection technique that removes linear effects of known tabular features from embeddings and extend it to high-order and interaction effects. In this way, we remove the effects of interpretable biochemical features from PLM embeddings. In an ablation study, we show that this leads to a decrease in performance for a downstream classifier trained only on the embeddings to predict protein fitness. In an additional evaluation, we find that these biochemical features explain a substantial part of the variance in the predictions of this classifier. Hence, we can show that PLM embeddings encode patterns correlated with biochemical properties and quantify their contribution to predicting protein fitness. This computationally efficient approach is not limited to the features or embeddings considered here and is readily transferable to problem settings beyond protein fitness prediction.

</details>


### [293] [Beyond Optimal Rates in Stochastic Optimization: Trajectory-Adaptive Stopping Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25551)
*Liviu Aolaritei,Lucas Lévy,Francis Bach,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对强凸随机优化中的SGD，构建了沿轨迹自适应、可同时对所有时间成立的置信上界序列（confidence sequences），用于最后一轮迭代到最优解的距离平方及加权平均的次优性，使SGD能够在统计有效性不受损的前提下，一旦达到指定精度即自适应停止，而非依赖先验的确定性horizon。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGD的传统分析在算法运行前就选定确定性horizon，但实际中的停止决策往往是通过观察演化轨迹自适应做出的，二者不匹配产生了根本性的认证问题：固定时间（fixed-time）的保证在数据依赖的停止时间处通常不再成立，而基于最坏情形界推导的确定性horizon又过于保守。

Method: 将SGD的演化轨迹视为一个顺序实验，观测值提供了关于未知优化误差的证据；为此开发了新的递归置信序列技术，以及适用于条件均值随时间变化、可预测范围可能无界增长的适应过程的一般化时间一致经验Bernstein不等式。这些置信序列完全可观测、具有轨迹自适应性，可同时对所有时间成立，并能扩展到小批量（minibatch）SGD，其中经验Bernstein界利用了每个小批量内部已实现二阶矩结构。

Result: 所构建的界在最坏情形下达到最优的1/t衰减率（直至迭代对数因子），并自适应于实际实现的随机梯度；数值实验表明，由此得到的停止规则所需的迭代次数可比自然的确定性horizon少若干个数量级。

Conclusion: 本文建立了SGD自适应停止的严格统计框架：通过轨迹自适应的时间一致置信序列，既保证了数据依赖停止时间处结果仍具有统计有效性，又充分利用了实际梯度信息，大幅减少不必要的迭代计算，为强凸随机优化中按需认证精度的自适应停止提供了理论与算法支撑。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is typically analyzed at a deterministic horizon chosen before the algorithm is run, even though practical stopping decisions are made adaptively by inspecting the evolving trajectory. This mismatch creates a fundamental certification problem: fixed-time guarantees do not generally remain valid at data-dependent stopping times, while deterministic horizons derived from worst-case bounds can be highly conservative. We address this problem for strongly convex stochastic optimization by constructing fully observable, trajectory-adaptive upper confidence sequences for the squared distance of the last iterate to the optimizer and the suboptimality of a weighted average. These bounds hold simultaneously over time, attain the optimal $1/t$ decay rate up to iterated-logarithmic factors in the worst case, and adapt to the realized stochastic gradients, allowing SGD to stop as soon as a prescribed accuracy is certified without sacrificing statistical validity. Our approach treats the evolving SGD trajectory as a sequential experiment whose observations provide evidence about the unknown optimization error. To formalize this perspective, we develop new recursive confidence-sequence techniques and a general time-uniform empirical Bernstein inequality for adapted processes with time-varying conditional means and predictable ranges that may grow without bound. We further extend these confidence-sequence constructions to minibatch SGD, with the empirical Bernstein bounds exploiting the realized second-moment structure within each minibatch. Numerical experiments show that the resulting stopping rules can require several orders of magnitude fewer iterations than natural deterministic horizons.

</details>


### [294] [Physics-Informed Foresight Pruning for Sparse PINN Solvers of Nonlinear PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25564)
*Ahmad Ishaque Karimi,Uvini Balasuriya Mudiyanselage,Kookjin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏 PirateNet PDE 求解器在初始化阶段的剪枝问题，提出物理信息谱感知剪枝（PI-SAP），通过 PDE 残差敏感性分配参数重要性，并在多种方程上验证其能更稳定地保持残差保真度，但没有任何剪枝准则在所有方程和稀疏度下普遍最优，因此需要显式平衡解侧与残差侧训练动态的剪枝方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN 通常依赖过度参数化模型来优化耦合的解目标与微分残差目标，导致所需容量及剪枝应保留什么尚不明确；标准 NTK 谱感知剪枝（NTK-SAP）旨在保持输出侧训练动态，但可能忽略主要通过控制方程中的导数发挥影响的重要参数。

Method: 提出物理信息谱感知剪枝（PI-SAP），利用 PDE 残差的敏感性来赋予参数显著度；并在 Gray-Scott 方程、复 Ginzburg-Landau 方程、Burgers 方程和线性对流方程上开展实验，同时结合小批量 PINN-NTK 诊断进行分析。

Result: 实验表明 PI-SAP 在 Gray-Scott 残差保真度上更稳定，且在激进稀疏度下具有竞争力；但没有任何准则在所有方程或稀疏度水平上统一最优。小批量 PINN-NTK 诊断显示残差保真度、解精度与核条件数是相互独立的目标。

Conclusion: 残差保真度、解精度与核条件数是不同目标，结论是应设计在优化过程中显式平衡解侧与残差侧训练动态的剪枝方法。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) often rely on over-parameterized models to optimize coupled solution and differential-residual objectives, leaving unclear how much capacity is necessary and what pruning should preserve. We study foresight pruning at initialization for sparse PirateNet PDE solvers. Standard neural tangent kernel spectrum-aware pruning (NTK-SAP) aims to preserve output-side training dynamics but may overlook parameters whose main influence arises through derivatives in the governing equations. We introduce physics-informed spectrum-aware pruning (PI-SAP), which assigns saliency using sensitivity of the PDE residual. Experiments on the Gray-Scott equations, complex Ginzburg-Landau equation, Burgers' equation, and linear convection equation show that PI-SAP more consistently preserves Gray-Scott residual fidelity and is competitive under aggressive sparsity. However, no criterion is uniformly optimal across equations or sparsity levels. Small-batch PINN-NTK diagnostics further show that residual fidelity, solution accuracy, and kernel conditioning are distinct objectives, motivating pruning methods that explicitly balance solution-side and residual-side training dynamics during optimization.

</details>


### [295] [Beyond Scaling: Self-Evolving LLM Agents for Hardware Kernel Optimization via an Experience-Driven Workflow and Experience Graph Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25570)
*Siyuan Chen,Runlin Hou,Shenxiu Wu,Yansong Sun,Junming Cao,Yiyu Zhang,Shudi Shao,Junhao Qiu,Zhichao Lu,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KOPE 是一个面向硬件内核优化的经验驱动框架，通过 Experience Graph Memory 记录优化轨迹（含正确性与性能反馈），并利用 Active Context Management and Injection 在固定 token 预算下检索相关经验，使智能体能够跨任务持续学习，显著超越最强基线 CANNBot。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件内核优化需要反复编译、正确性测试、性能分析和修订。尽管更强的基座模型、更长的上下文窗口和更长的执行周期提升了单次任务的优化效果，但这些进展本身无法让智能体从已完成的优化过程中学习。现有内核优化智能体很少保存"某个决策、其观测到的执行反馈、以及之后基于该证据做出的决策"，而保留全部历史轨迹又不现实——不断膨胀的历史会挤占当前任务的上下文空间。因此缺乏一种能跨任务保留并复用优化经验的外部记忆机制。

Method: KOPE 框架包含两大核心组件：1）Experience Graph Memory：以图结构记录优化轨迹，保留决策顺序、观测结果和备选分支，使在目标硬件上收集到的证据（正确性与性能反馈）能用于后续优化步骤和其他任务；2）Active Context Management and Injection：在固定的 token 预算约束下主动检索并注入相关经验，替代被动地由智能体自行构造上下文。

Result: 在相同的 GLM-5.2 设置下，KOPE 的每算子加速比几何均值是最强基线 CANNBot 的 1.54 倍。在完整的 53 算子消融中：Active Context Management and Injection 将通过率从 60.0% 提升至 84.6%，评估器报告的正值域几何均值从 0.0382 提升至 0.0661，并将优化 token 消耗从 15.9B 降至 1.113B（相对被动式上下文构建）；启用 Experience Graph Memory 将全套件通过率从 55.2% 提升至 84.6%，并在有效时序比较上取得 1.43 倍的几何均值加速。

Conclusion: KOPE 证明：在基座模型保持固定不变的情况下，通过外部经验记忆与主动上下文管理即可实现跨任务、跨步骤的持续优化学习，既显著提升优化质量与正确性，又大幅降低 token 开销，为内核优化的可积累、可复用经验提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Hardware kernel optimization requires repeated compilation, correctness testing, profiling, and revision. LLM agents can automate parts of this process, and stronger foundation models, longer context windows, and longer execution horizons have improved optimization within individual tasks. These advances alone do not enable an agent to learn from completed optimization runs. Existing kernel-optimization agents seldom preserve a decision, its observed execution feedback, and the later decisions that use that evidence. Retaining every prior trajectory is also impractical because an expanding history competes with the current task for context. We present KOPE, an experience-driven framework for hardware kernel optimization. KOPE records optimization trajectories with correctness and performance feedback in Experience Graph Memory, then uses Active Context Management and Injection to retrieve relevant experience under a fixed token budget. The graph retains decision order, observed outcomes, and alternative branches, allowing evidence collected on the target hardware to inform later optimization steps and tasks. Under the same GLM-5.2 setting, the geometric mean of KOPE's per-operator speedups is $1.54\times$ that of CANNBot, the strongest competing baseline. In a complete 53-operator ablation, Active Context Management and Injection raises pass rate from 60.0\% to 84.6\%, increases the evaluator-reported positive-field geometric mean from 0.0382 to 0.0661, and reduces optimization token consumption from 15.9B to 1.113B tokens relative to passive agent-led context construction. Enabling Experience Graph Memory raises full-suite pass rate from 55.2\% to 84.6\% and yields a $1.43\times$ geometric-mean speedup on valid timing comparisons. These results support continual optimization through external experience while the foundation model remains fixed.

</details>


### [296] [Individual Fairness in Hierarchical Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25586)
*Binita Maity,Shrutimoy Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究层次聚类中的个体公平性约束，即在局部k近邻邻域内限制相对失真。作者将该约束形式化为受控超度量（dominated ultrametrics）上的可行性问题，刻画了可行性所需的最小乘性松弛，揭示了尖锐的局部阈值、有界扰动下的稳定性、关于k的单调性，并证明了局部可实现性与全局可实现性之间存在Θ(log n)的本质差距。实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次聚类生成的超度量表示施加了很强的全局几何约束，可能导致局部相似性被扭曲，从而不成比例地影响个别数据点。现有方法缺乏对个体层面（而非全局或平均层面）公平性的保证，因此需要研究在局部k近邻邻域内约束相对失真的层次聚类可行性问题。

Method: 将个体公平性要求形式化为受控超度量上的可行性问题；通过刻画满足约束所需的最小乘性松弛来判定可行性；从理论上分析局部阈值（sharp local threshold）、在有限扰动下的稳定性、对参数k的单调性，并对比局部与全局可实现性之间的差距（证明Θ(log n)分离），最后通过合成与真实数据集实验予以验证。

Result: 确定了可行性的尖锐局部阈值；证明了可行性在有界扰动下保持稳定；建立了可行性关于k的单调性；证明了局部可实现性与全局可实现性之间存在Θ(log n)的本质性分离。

Conclusion: 个体公平性视角下，层次聚类在局部k近邻邻域内的可行性具有清晰的阈值刻画与稳定性保证，且局部约束与全局约束存在本质差异；理论结果在合成与真实数据上均得到实验支持。

Abstract: Hierarchical clustering produces ultrametric representations that impose strong global geometric constraints and may distort local similarities in ways that disproportionately affect individual data points. We study hierarchical clustering under an individual fairness requirement that bounds relative distortion within local $k$-nearest neighborhoods. We formulate this requirement as a feasibility problem over dominated ultrametrics and characterize the minimal multiplicative slack required for feasibility. We identify a sharp local threshold, prove stability under bounded perturbations, establish monotonicity in $k$, and show an intrinsic $Θ(\log n)$ separation between local and global realizability. Experiments on synthetic and real world datasets support our theoretical results.

</details>


### [297] [M-Fibration Theory with Applications to Neural Network Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25598)
*Paolo Boldi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用且全面的理论框架，用于处理以交换幺半群为标签的图上的纤维化（fibration），这是对经典图纤维化理论的真正推广，能够处理带权图以及带有其他代数结构标签的图，并自然衍生了近似纤维化的概念。作为应用示例，作者展示了该框架如何用于任意神经网络（包括CNN）的压缩，为几何深度学习中的纤维化对称性研究提供了坚实的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典图纤维化理论只能处理无标签或简单标签的图，无法直接适用于带权图（如带权神经网络）或带有其他代数结构标签的图；此外，实际应用中需要一种自然的方式来处理近似纤维化，而现有框架缺乏这样的支撑。

Method: 作者构建了一个以交换幺半群为标签的图上的纤维化一般性理论框架，将经典图纤维化概念推广到带权图及更广泛的代数结构标签情形，并在此框架内自然引入近似纤维化概念。文中通过将该框架应用于任意神经网络（包括CNN）的压缩来予以验证。

Result: 该框架成功推广了现有图纤维化理论，统一处理带权图和多种代数结构标签，支持近似纤维化的形式化刻画，并为神经网络（含CNN）的压缩提供了严格的理论依据，支撑了此前关于纤维化对称性在几何深度学习中作用的研究结果。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了一个普适的带标签图纤维化理论，扩展了其适用范围并建立了与近似纤维化的桥梁，同时为神经网络压缩提供了坚实的理论基础，表明该框架在深度学习与图论交叉领域具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: The purpose of this paper is to provide a general, comprehensive, theoretical framework that allows one to deal with fibrations on graphs labelled on a commutative monoid. This is a genuine extension of the theory of graph fibrations (as introduced in "Fibrations of Graphs" [Discrete Math., vol. 243, pp. 21-66, 2002]), that makes it possible to deal with weighted graphs, and also graphs labelled with other algebraic structures. The derived theory also lends itself naturally to consider approximate fibrations. As an example, we show how this framework can be applied to the compression of arbitrary neural networks (including CNNs), providing a strong theoretical underpinning to the recent results in "The role of fibration symmetries in geometric deep learning" [Proc. Natl. Acad. Sci. USA, vol. 123, no. 4, p. e2416552123, 2026]

</details>


### [298] [Frequency-aware forecasting for short-term typhoon gust prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25604)
*Xuefei Wang,Tingyi Liu,Heng Zhang,Shengjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了WDANet，一种面向台风条件下阵风预测的频率感知深度学习框架，通过平稳小波分解、特征级线性调制（FiLM）策略和双分支编码器-解码器架构，将趋势分量与波动分量分开建模，在24小时预测范围内于短提前期表现出优势，并在极端大风事件中优于ECMWF-HRES的RMSE和MAE指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 台风条件下阵风具有高度非平稳和多尺度特征，现有深度学习模型难以同时捕捉长期趋势与快速局部变化，导致极端事件下预测性能下降。

Method: 提出WDANet频率感知预测框架，集成平稳小波分解、特征级线性调制（FiLM）策略和双分支编码器-解码器架构，对趋势成分和波动成分进行分离建模；以中国西太平洋近海区域为例开展细网格阵风预测研究。

Result: 在24小时预测时间范围内，WDANet在短提前期下具有优势，前6小时预测精度优于ECMWF-HRES；在极端大风事件中能更准确捕捉阵风峰值，并取得最优的RMSE和MAE表现。

Conclusion: WDANet在台风条件下阵风预测中展现出优越性，对近海风电运行、灾害预警和风险减缓具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Accurate gust forecasting under typhoon conditions remains challenging due to the highly non-stationary and multi-scale characteristics of extreme wind fluctuations. Existing deep learning models often struggle to simultaneously capture long-term trends and rapid local variations, resulting in degraded performance during extreme events. We propose WDANet, a frequency-aware forecasting framework that integrates stationary wavelet decomposition, a Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) strategy, and a dual-branch encoder-decoder architecture, enabling separate modeling of trend and fluctuation components. Taking the offshore regions of the Western Pacific in China as an example, we conduct fine-grid wind gust prediction research. The results demonstrate that WDANet shows advantages for short lead times under the experimental setting across a 24-h forecasting horizon and achieves higher prediction accuracy than ECMWF-HRES within the first 6 h. During extreme wind events, WDANet more accurately captures gust peaks and attains the best RMSE and MAE performance. These results highlight its potential for offshore wind power operation, disaster warning, and risk mitigation.

</details>


### [299] [DCEO: Direct Causal Effect Optimization for Long-Term User Value Modeling in E-commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25635)
*Junzhao Zhang,Tao Zhang,Liren Yu,Feiyi Dong,Zhixuan Zhang,Dan Ou,Haihong Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DCEO 是一个面向工业级电商搜索排序的数据驱动框架，通过相对因果效应衡量用户级代理指标与长期目标的对齐程度，并利用 actor-critic 架构动态生成上下文相关的多目标融合权重，从而学习与用户级长期目标（如 GMV）更对齐的商品级代理分数；离线实验验证了其有效性与可解释性，线上 A/B 测试中 GMV 较传统代理提升 0.36%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商搜索排序的真实优化目标是用户级长期目标（如 N 日累计购买或人均 GMV），但排序本身基于每次请求内的商品级分数，存在粒度不一致问题。现有方法通过人工设计的多目标融合（对点击、加购、购买、交易金额等商品级目标预测做加权）来近似最终目标，这种手工融合依赖少量人工调参权重，缺乏细粒度个性化，与最终目标的对齐效果欠佳。

Method: 首先将商品级代理分数聚合成用户级代理指标，并用相对因果效应量化该指标与最终目标的对齐程度；随后构建 actor-critic 框架：critic 在给定用户级代理指标时估计最终目标，actor 动态生成上下文相关的多目标融合权重以构造商品级代理分数，并直接以最大化相对因果效应为目标进行训练。

Result: 大量离线实验与分析证明了 DCEO 的有效性和可解释性；该框架已部署于大规模工业级电商搜索系统，在 41 天线上 A/B 测试中较传统 GMV 代理在 GMV 上提升 0.36%。

Conclusion: DCEO 通过数据驱动方式替代人工设计的静态多目标融合，实现了商品级代理分数与用户级长期目标之间的更优对齐，兼具个性化、可解释性与实际部署收益，为工业搜索排序中代理目标设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Industrial e-commerce search systems ultimately aim to optimize the user-level long-term objective, such as n-day cumulative purchases or gross merchandise value (GMV) per user. However, such objectives are defined at the user level, whereas search ranking is based on item-level scores within each request. Existing methods typically bridge this granularity gap through manually designed multi-objective fusion, where predictions of multiple item-level objectives, such as clicks, carts, purchases, and transaction value, are combined into a ranking score that serves as a proxy for the ultimate objective. Such hand-crafted fusion schemes rely on a small set of manually tuned weights, limiting fine-grained personalization and leading to suboptimal alignment with the ultimate objective. In this paper, we propose DCEO (Direct Causal Effect Optimization), a data-driven framework for learning item-level proxy scores that are better aligned with the ultimate objective. We first aggregate the item-level proxy scores into a user-level proxy metric and quantify its alignment with the ultimate objective using a relative causal effect. We then develop an actor-critic framework, where the critic estimates the ultimate objective for a given user-level proxy metric, and the actor dynamically generates context-dependent fusion weights over multiple objectives to construct the item-level proxy scores and is trained to directly optimize the relative causal effect. Extensive offline experiments and analyses demonstrate the effectiveness and interpretability of DCEO. In addition, DCEO has been deployed in a large-scale industrial e-commerce search system, outperforming the conventional GMV proxy by 0.36% in GMV in a 41-day online A/B test.

</details>


### [300] [A Token-Level Analysis of Sampled-Token Reverse-KL On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25643)
*Bing Shao,Jiazheng Zhang,Long Ma,Yujiong Shen,Senjie Jin,Xin Guo,Yuming Yang,Mingxu Chai,Zhiheng Xi,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对基于K2估计量的on-policy蒸馏（反向KL）进行了梯度级分析，发现每个token梯度的ℓ1范数可分解为“教师-学生对数概率差”与“学生侧softmax因子”之积，且该范数在token间高度不均匀：学生低概率token占据总梯度范数的过大部分并伴随较大的师生差距。基于此提出Surprise-aware Reweighting（SuRe）这一轻量加权方法，在两个Qwen3规模上提升了数学指标且无明显领域外退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD虽用冻结教师对学生的自生成轨迹提供token级信号，但采样损失如何跨token分配梯度更新尚不清楚；作者希望从梯度层面揭示反向KL蒸馏中token间更新的非均匀性，从而指导更有效的蒸馏干预。

Method: （1）推导per-token K2估计器下反向KL关于学生logits的梯度，证明其ℓ1范数可分解为|师生对数概率差|与随采样token在学生侧概率降低而增大的softmax因子之积；（2）在数学蒸馏实验中量化这些per-token范数的高度非均匀性；（3）据此提出SuRe：一种detached、有界的加权规则，进一步放大已有的对低概率token的梯度分配。

Result: 在两种Qwen3学生规模下，SuRe相较vanilla OPD在多项数学指标上取得提升，且在选定的领域外基准上无明显退化；同时实证验证了per-token梯度范数的高度非均匀性及其与师生差距的富集关系。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是对以K2估计器训练的反向KL OPD给出梯度层级的刻画，并以其为指导提出SuRe作为经验性实现；该工作增进了对OPD梯度分配机制的理解，并为更精细的蒸馏加权提供了依据。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) supervises a student on its own trajectories with token-level signals from a frozen teacher, yet how a sampled loss allocates updates across tokens remains poorly understood. We analyze the gradient of the per-token K2 estimator of reverse KL with respect to the student logits. The $\ell_1$ norm of this gradient factorizes into the absolute teacher--student log-probability gap and a student-side softmax factor that grows as the sampled token becomes less likely under the student. In our math-distillation runs, these per-token norms are highly non-uniform: low-student-probability tokens account for a disproportionate share of their sum and are also enriched in large teacher--student gaps. As a lightweight intervention suggested by this analysis, we study Surprise-aware Reweighting (SuRe), a detached, bounded weighting rule that further amplifies this existing allocation. Across two Qwen3 student scales, SuRe improves several math metrics over vanilla OPD and shows no clear degradation on the selected out-of-domain benchmarks. Our primary contribution is therefore a gradient-level characterization of reverse-KL OPD trained with the K2 estimator, with SuRe as one empirical instantiation.

</details>


### [301] [LDAC-Net: A Learnable Multi-Lag Differencing Attention-Convolution Network for Drift-Robust Recognition with Low-Cost MOX Gas Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25646)
*Xin Zhang,Liangxiu Han,Yue Shi,Tam Sobeih*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LDAC-Net，一种端到端可学习的多滞后差分注意力-卷积网络，直接处理多通道金属氧化物（MOX）气体传感器信号，用于气体与气味识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本MOX气体传感器信号受慢化学瞬态、漂移的传感器偏移、尺度变化和跨通道相关性影响；现有流程普遍使用固定的二阶（此处应为一阶，原文为first-order）时间差分预处理（FOTD），需要手动选择滞后且可能丢弃有用的响应信息。

Method: LDAC-Net包含两大部分：一是可学习的差分特征增强前端，由窗口条件统计仿射归一化（补偿窗口特定的偏移与尺度变化）与可学习多滞后差分（对多个滞后的时间差异进行加权组合）组成；二是紧凑的注意力-卷积主干，用于建模局部瞬态与更长时间范围的时间依赖。

Result: 在50类SmellNet-Base任务上取得68.2% top-1准确率，比最优FOTD预处理对比模型高约14个百分点，比原始输入Transformer高30个百分点以上；迁移到SmellNet-Mixtures将准确率从45.4%提升至50.5%；在62通道eNose-Drift基准强长期漂移下取得70.6% top-1准确率和69.6% macro-F1，分别比使用数据重调优FOTD的最优对比模型高8.0和3.0个百分点。消融实验证实两个组件均有贡献。

Conclusion: 可学习、传感器感知的预处理比固定手工设计的差分方法对低成本MOX气体传感器识别更有效，LDAC-Net在多个基准上实现了显著的性能提升与良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Portable electronic-nose systems based on low-cost metal-oxide (MOX) gas sensors offer a practical solution for gas and odour recognition, but their signals are affected by slow chemical transients, drifting sensor offsets, scale variation, and cross-channel correlations. Existing pipelines commonly use fixed first-order temporal differencing (FOTD), which requires a manually selected lag and may discard useful response information. We propose LDAC-Net, an end-to-end learnable multi-lag differencing attention-convolution network that operates directly on multi-channel MOX signals. Its learnable differential feature enhancement front-end combines window-conditioned statistical affine normalisation, which compensates for window-specific offset and scale variation, with learnable multi-lag differencing, which weights and combines temporal differences across multiple lags. A compact attention-convolution backbone subsequently models local transients and longer-range temporal dependencies. On the 50-class SmellNet-Base task, LDAC-Net achieves 68.2% top-1 accuracy, exceeding the best FOTD-preprocessed comparison model by approximately 14 percentage points and the raw-input Transformer by more than 30 points. Ablation studies confirm the contributions of both proposed components. The representation also transfers to SmellNet-Mixtures, improving accuracy from 45.4% to 50.5%, and generalises to the 62-channel eNose-Drift benchmark under strong long-term drift, achieving 70.6% top-1 accuracy and 69.6% macro-F1. These results outperform the best comparison model with dataset-retuned FOTD preprocessing by 8.0 and 3.0 points, respectively, demonstrating that learnable, sensor-aware preprocessing is more effective than fixed handcrafted differencing for low-cost MOX gas-sensor recognition.

</details>


### [302] [Adversarial Training of Linear Models under Stealthy Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25681)
*Lovisa Eriksson,Dave Zachariah,André M. H. Teixeira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于检测器的切换模型，在考虑隐蔽性最优攻击的条件下，为线性预测模型推导出对抗风险的凸优化形式，并通过攻击概率超参数在干净数据与受攻击数据之间实现显式的性能权衡，仿真实验表明即使在攻击概率误设时也能提升部分受攻击数据上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测模型广泛使用但易受虚假数据注入攻击；现有检测方案和对抗训练缺乏对隐蔽攻击的保证，无法从理论上应对能够绕过检测器的最优攻击。

Method: 提出一种由检测器驱动的切换（switched）模型，其设计使得最优攻击策略天然具备隐蔽性；针对线性预测模型，将由此产生的对抗风险推导为凸优化问题，并在模型中引入受保护特征以及刻画攻击概率的超参数。

Result: 在真实与合成数据上的数值仿真显示，所提方法在部分受攻击的数据上性能更优，且即使攻击概率被误设（misspecified）时依然保持改进效果。

Conclusion: 基于检测器的切换模型能够为隐蔽攻击提供理论层面对抗风险的凸刻画，并通过可调节的超参数实现干净/受攻击场景间的显式权衡，具有较强的鲁棒性与实用价值。

Abstract: Predictive models are widely used in many fields, but are vulnerable to false data injection attacks. To address this, detection schemes and adversarial training have been proposed, but such approaches lack guarantees against stealthy attacks. We therefore propose a detector-based switched model, in which optimal attack strategies are stealthy. For linear prediction models, we derive a convex formulation of the resulting adversarial risk. The model incorporates protected features and introduces a hyperparameter modelling attack probability, enabling an explicit performance trade-off between clean and attacked data regimes. Numerical simulations on real and synthetic data show improved performance on partially attacked data, even for misspecified attack probabilities.

</details>


### [303] [Modeling spatio-temporal locality in multi-step forecasting of geo-referenced time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25698)
*Annunziata D'Aversa,Gianvito Pio,Michelangelo Ceci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SPALT，一种基于线性模型树的时空局部性建模方法，用于同时预测多个地理分布式传感器未来多个时间步的测量值，并在可再生能源发电预测的 3 个真实数据集上验证了其优于树模型和融合时空维度的先进神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理分布式传感器数据因空间自相关现象在邻近位置间产生相互依赖，不能独立处理；现有方法虽能捕捉这类现象，但通常对所有位置进行全局的空间建模，忽略了仅发生在相似趋势时间序列之间的时空局部性关系。

Method: SPALT 利用线性模型树在局部刻画空间自相关：在树的构建过程中，启发式方法将具有相似趋势的时间序列分入同一节点，并有选择地注入考虑空间维度的额外特征；同时提出一种基于 Reduced Error Pruning 的新剪枝策略，在树简化阶段也考虑时空局部性；该方法面向多步预测设置，可同时为多个传感器的多个未来时间步提供预测。

Result: 在 3 个真实世界数据集（多个可再生能源电站的短期能量生产数据）上的实验表明，SPALT 在不同预测时间范围内都能有效预测能源产量，且性能优于基于树的模型以及同时融合时间与空间维度的先进神经网络。

Conclusion: SPALT 通过局部建模空间自相关关系，在测量值来自分布式传感器的场景（尤其是可再生能源发电预测）中能提供显著收益，是一种有效的时空局部性预测方法。

Abstract: Forecasting future measurements from geographically distributed sensors is essential across many domains. However, the spatial distribution of these sensors raises multiple challenges, primarily due to spatial autocorrelation phenomena, that introduce inter-dependencies among nearby locations, that cannot therefore be treated independently. While some existing approaches can capture such phenomena, they generally model the spatial dimension globally across all locations. On the other hand, the method we propose in this paper, called SPALT, focuses on capturing spatial relationships among time series with similar trends, even if they occur at different times, thus modeling the spatio-temporal locality. SPALT leverages linear model trees, which allow us to consider the spatial autocorrelation locally: during the tree-building process, the adopted heuristics group time series exhibiting similar trends into the same node, on which additional features considering the spatial dimension are selectively injected. Additionally, we propose a new pruning strategy, based on Reduced Error Pruning, that also considers the spatio-temporal locality during the tree simplification. Designed for a multi-step setting, SPALT provides forecasts for multiple future time steps across multiple sensors simultaneously. The characteristics exhibited by SPALT can provide significant benefits in different domains, where measurements come from distributed sensors. In this paper, we focus on data produced by sensors located in multiple renewable power plants measuring their energy production at regular, short intervals. Experiments on 3 real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of SPALT in forecasting the production of energy at different time horizons, and its superior performance in comparison with tree-based models and state-of-the-art neural networks that incorporate both temporal and spatial dimensions.

</details>


### [304] [Tropospheric temperature and humidity profile retrieval from Meteosat Flexible Combined Imager based on deep learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25700)
*Alejandro Salgueiro,Johannes Rausch,Julie Thérèse Villinger,Angela Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于空间感知深度学习（Residual U-Net）的框架，在不使用数值天气预报（NWP）背景场作为输入的前提下，直接从MTG-FCI全部16个通道的观测中反演全天空对流层温度和湿度廓线。模型在14个月的FCI与CERRA再分析样本上训练，并使用独立无线电探空数据验证，温度偏差低于0.4 K、标准差1.5–1.9 K，相对湿度标准差12–20%，在云下性能仅小幅退化，表明该方法可独立于NWP实现快速自主的大气监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MTG-FCI虽然相较前代具有更高的时空分辨率和更广的光谱覆盖，但从宽波段成像仪反演垂直廓线本质上是病态/困难的问题；现有业务算法通常依赖NWP背景场来弥补有限的红外光谱分辨率，从而削弱了反演结果的独立性。作者希望开发一种不依赖预报背景场、能够从FCI观测中独立提取对流层温湿廓线的方案，以实现更快速自主的大气监测。

Method: 采用空间感知的深度学习框架：以Residual U-Net为骨干，利用全部16个FCI通道的空间上下文信息进行反演。训练数据为14个月欧洲上空FCI观测与CERRA再分析目标的配对样本，无预报廓线作为输入。通过独立无线电探空进行验证，并开展消融实验（考察空间上下文的影响）和特征敏感性分析（考察各通道贡献）。

Result: 独立无线电探空验证显示：温度偏差低于0.4 K，标准差为1.5–1.9 K；相对湿度标准差为12–20%（CERRA为9–19%）。云下的性能退化有限，云顶下方标准差增幅低于0.4 K和3 % RH。消融实验表明空间上下文显著提升反演质量，尤其是云顶下方增益最大；特征敏感性分析与FCI各波段已知辐射传输特性总体一致，可见光和近红外通道也有贡献。

Conclusion: 空间感知的深度学习模型能够从静止卫星成像仪观测中提取统计上可靠的对流层温湿廓线，且不依赖NWP预报场，从而支持更快速、自主的大气监测。该方法在云下仍保持稳健，验证了利用空间卷积捕获辐射传输信息以弥补单像素光谱信息不足的可行性。

Abstract: The Meteosat Third Generation (MTG) Flexible Combined Imager (FCI) offers new opportunities for tropospheric temperature and humidity profiling, at higher spatio-temporal resolutions and expanded spectral coverage relative to its predecessor. Vertically resolved retrievals from broadband imagers are inherently challenging, and operational retrieval algorithms typically rely on numerical weather prediction (NWP) background fields to compensate for limited infrared spectral resolution, reducing the retrievals' independence. We develop a spatially aware deep learning framework to retrieve all-sky tropospheric temperature and humidity profiles from FCI, without forecast profiles as input. A Residual U-Net that exploits spatial context across all 16 FCI channels was trained on 14 months of collocated FCI observations and CERRA reanalysis targets over Europe. Validated against independent radiosondes, retrieved temperatures show biases below 0.4 K and standard deviations of 1.5-1.9 K. Retrieved relative humidity standard deviations range from 12-20 %, compared to 9-19 % for CERRA. Performance degrades modestly under clouds, with standard deviation increases below 0.4 K and 3 % RH beneath cloud tops despite limited direct radiative information. Ablation experiments show that spatial context improves retrievals, with the largest gains below cloud tops. Feature sensitivity analysis indicates broad consistency with FCI bands' established radiative transfer characteristics. Visible and near-infrared channels contribute despite not being commonly used in physics-based profile inversions. These results demonstrate that spatially aware deep learning models can extract statistically reliable tropospheric profiles from geostationary imager observations, independent of NWP forecast fields, enabling more rapid autonomous monitoring of the atmosphere.

</details>


### [305] [Fairness-Aware Test-Time Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25707)
*Yoann Launay,Parameswaran Kamalaruban,Tom Kempton,Stuart Burrell,David Sutton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究CLIP分类在子群体偏移下，逐片段测试时自适应（episodic TTA）对公平性的影响，并提出FairTPT方法——通过软提示调优联合最小化目标边际熵、最大化虚假相关边际熵来实现公平性感知的测试时自适应，同时通过监测目标损失防止模型崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型在关键应用中广泛部署，且经济与工程约束禁止重新训练或微调；但这些模型可能对受保护人口群体表现出系统性偏差，而现有去偏方法需要大规模重训练且依赖人口统计属性。因此亟需在分布偏移下改善预训练模型公平性的测试时自适应方法。

Method: 提出FairTPT，一种新型公平性感知的逐片段TTA方法：通过软提示调优联合最小化目标（target）边际熵并最大化虚假相关（spurious）边际熵；并通过在线性区间内监测测试时目标损失的变化来防止过度盲化导致的灾难性遗忘。

Result: 实验发现：标准逐片段TTA通常会加剧多数群体与少数群体之间的差异；在不降低目标任务性能的前提下使模型对虚假属性"盲化"本质上是困难的；过度盲化会导致灾难性遗忘。FairTPT在保持整体性能的同时取得公平性改进，优于所有现有最优的逐片段测试时去偏方法。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了标准TTA对公平性的负面影响并分析了模型崩溃机制，FairTPT为稳健测试时自适应奠定了基础，是实践中实现公平性的关键。

Abstract: Vision-language models have displayed remarkable capabilities in multi-modal understanding and are increasingly used in critical applications where economic and practical deployment constraints prohibit re-training or fine-tuning. However, these models can also exhibit systematic biases that disproportionately affect protected demographic groups and existing approaches to addressing these biases require extensive model retraining and access to demographic attributes. There is a clear need to develop test-time adaptation (TTA) approaches that improve the fairness characteristics of pretrained models under distributional shift. In this paper, we evaluate how episodic TTA affects fairness in CLIP classification under subpopulation shifts and develop FairTPT, a novel fairness-aware episodic TTA method that jointly minimizes target marginal entropy while maximizing spurious marginal entropy through soft-prompt tuning. We find that standard episodic TTA generally exacerbates disparities between majority and minority groups, that blinding a model to spurious attributes without degrading target performance is inherently challenging, and that excessive blinding can lead to catastrophic forgetting.
